Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the hamiltonian framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 642. The resulting operator achieves bound O(209/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on hamiltonian structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 642. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 209. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
